Let's design search robustly:
- Use local optimizer (mirror descent / gradient) but with diverse starts: sqrt, sqrt^p, beta, random low-frequency, and length variations.
- Add global restarts: perturb best with high-temperature random log noise, then reoptimize.
- Use active-set/constraint-aware direction: Instead of just power weights, optimize M explicitly with a barrier for all b_k. Could use a smoothed maximum (softmax) of b_k with temperature, gradient. Softmax handles all constraints, not just near max, and may move more globally. Previous used power weights; we can use logsumexp of b_k / τ to smooth. Objective M ≈ τ log∑exp(b_k/τ). Since b_k scale O(1). Minimize 2n M/S^2. Gradient of logsumexp: weighted b with weights exp(b_k/τ)/Z. If τ small, near max; if larger, influences tail. To reduce max, tail near max matters; choose τ adaptively. This could find descent toward flatten all top coefficients. Implement in log-domain mirror descent.

- Use normalization with log-sum-exp? Keep sum constant by subtracting mean gradient or using softmax normalization. The objective scale invariant; optimizing log heights with fixed sum via softmax exactly makes sum target. Update z ← z - step * (grad - mean_grad) to preserve sum? But softmax targeting sum handles.

- Use exact local score via FFT. For gradient of logsumexp, need convolution with weights, same as power weights but weights = exp((b - bmax)/τ). We can compute weights from c, no need FFT for weights. Then gradient via FFT. Good. We can sweep τ (mode replaced by temperature). Also include L2 flattening direction for all coefficients: gradient of ∑ b_k^2? Since constraint max, secondary. L2 residual direction c - mean(c). For nonconvex, can use.

- Implement line search with backtracking and perhaps accept small improvements. Use multiple τ values from high to low: high temperature flattens all coefficients; low polish peaks. This is different from previous power weights q exponential; equivalent but τ continuous. Actually power weights (c/cmax)^q with q large approximates peak; high q same low temp. Previous schedule q 8,32,64, plus L2. It may have gotten stuck because always starts from sqrt and smooths? Need add global perturbations, random restarts, and maybe variable length. Also, previous local_score normalizes sum to sqrt(2n), but scale invariant; okay.

Could improve by using a more aggressive global algorithm: simulated annealing with log-noise and occasional "reverse mixing" or "shifting".

Maybe key is multi-start. Let's design final algorithm:

1. Define functions:
   - next_p2
   - centered_clipped logits
   - softmax_target
   - sqrt_series, beta_family, gamma_family, random smooth candidates
   - conv_fft, score_from_c
   - logsumexp_weights(c, temp): weights = exp((c - cmax)/temp), normalized; if temp inf uniform.
   - gradient_convolution(a, weights, size): as before.
   - local objective score.
   - optimizer: given initial a, time, rng, label. Use adaptive temperature schedule and step.

2. In optimizer, maintain best a, score, z. At each iteration:
   - c=conv(a)
   - compute weights using current temperature. Maybe temperature schedule alternates: start high (e.g., 0.2 if c scale ~1?), then lower. Since c normalized maybe max ~ score/(2n) ~0.0002 for n=4096; need temperature relative to c range. Better to scale y = c - cmin? Use normalized c: p = exp((c/cmax -1)/temp_unit) with temp_unit maybe 0.1? Previous power weights c/cmax ** q. q=1 -> linear, large q peak. We can set q random. Use p=(c/cmax)^q; if cmax=0. This works scale-invariant. Sweep q [1,4,16,64,256]. Also use softmax over slack: weights ∝ exp((c - cmax)/Δ) where Δ = (cmax - quantile?) but c/cmax^q simpler.
   - For L2 mode, use residual c - mean(c) or c - median active.
   - Direction d = -grad (for minimization). In log-domain update z -= step*d + maybe center to preserve sum? Since softmax target rescales, centering z may help.
   - Line search: try steps [step, step/2, 2step, step*0.25]. Score via FFT. Choose best.
   - If no improvement, reduce step, add random log noise.
   - Periodically re-evaluate exact local and maybe global best.

3. To escape local minima:
   - Have outer loop: pick a starting sequence.
   - Run optimizer with many restarts: after each optimizer segment from current best, if time remains, perturb with high temperature (e.g., add σ zeta, σ=0.1..1), rescale, run short optimizer. Accept if improved.
   - Use several initial families: sqrt, reversed sqrt, geometric r in [0.8,0.9,...], beta (t^{p-1}(1-t)^{q-1}) nonnegative, shifted sqrt, random smoothed (FFT band-limited), and stored height_sequence_1.

Detailed _optimize_run:
Input a_init normalized sum target=1. If a invalid, use sqrt.
Initialize z from log(a) center clipped, a=softmax.
Compute c,score. best_a, best_score, best_z.
Set q_schedule maybe cycled. We can use an internal phase list: [(mode,-1 or q, duration_frac?)]? For a run with max_seconds, we can simply dynamic choose mode based on iteration/fail:
   phase timer: cycle modes: L2 (0.5? weight signed), q1, q4, q16, q64, repeated every 1-2 seconds. This avoids prealloc.
Use a variable mode_idx, phase_time=2.0 seconds, mode_cycle = [-1, 1, 4, 16, 64, 256]. Maybe L2 more: [-1,-1,1,4,16,64,256]. Restart phase after mode change? No.
- step=0.2 initial.
- fail=0.
Loop until deadline:
   if phase_time expired, advance mode.
   compute c for current a? We maintain c from last candidate. But after random resets recompute.
   if mode == -1: weights = c - median/cmean. Use target = weighted average? We want minimize variance. Could use c - np.mean(c) (signed). To avoid very large dynamic, clip weights to +-cmax. Normalize gradient scale. Maybe use c - c_q where c_q = median of c for robust.
   else: q=mode; weights = (c/cmax)**q, normalized.
   g=gradient(a,weights,grad_size)
   scale=max(abs(g)) if mode -1 else max(g) (could 0). If scale small, break or reset.
   d=g/scale.
   For line search factors: [step, step/2, 2*step, step/4]. For each: zt = z - fac*d; if fail>10, add noise sigma=0.02+...; center_clip; at=softmax; ct=conv; sc=score. Choose best (lowest sc). If best_sc < score - 1e-12: update current z,a,c,score; step=min(2,max(0.02, step*1.1)); fail=0; if score<best...;
   else: step*=0.7; fail++; if fail>20: big random: z = best_z + rng.normal(0, sigma_reset), sigma_reset=0.2+0.5*min(fail/100,1); a=softmax; c=conv; score=score_from_c; (current score may >best). step=0.3; fail=0; mode_idx=random? continue.
   If fail>100: exit? Let phase continue but if no improvement for long, break to outer? In a single run, maybe break if no improvement for 60s. We can return best.

But current score may become worse after reset; line search from there. Need track global best separately.

Maybe better to have _optimize_run always try to improve from current; if fail too many, reset to best plus random and continue. This helps escape.

Optimizer:
```
def _optimize_run(a_init, max_seconds, rng, label, deadline):
    a = _normalize(a_init if valid else _sqrt_series(len), target=1.0)
    n=a.size
    if max_seconds <=1 or deadline passed return a, _local_score? maintain.
    conv_size=_next_p2(2*n-1); grad_size=_next_p2(3*n-2)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z=_center_clip_z(z); a=_softmax_target(z,1.0)
    c=_conv_fft(a,conv_size); score=_score_from_c(a,c)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score; best_z=z.copy()
    start=time.monotonic(); phase_end=start+2.0; mode_cycle=[-1,-1,1,4,16,64,256]; mode_idx=0; step=0.25; fail=0
    while time.monotonic() < min(start+max_seconds, deadline):
        if time.monotonic() >= phase_end:
            mode_idx=(mode_idx+1)%len(mode_cycle); phase_end=time.monotonic()+(2.0 if rng.random()<0.5 else 4.0)
        mode=mode_cycle[mode_idx]
        cmax=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
        if not finite or cmax <=1e-300: break
        if mode == -1:
            weights = c - float(np.mean(c))
            # maybe clip weights to [-cmax, cmax] to prevent tails zeros with large negative? zeros -mean ~ -mean small; okay.
        else:
            weights = _peak_weights(c, mode)
        g=_gradient(a, weights, grad_size)
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(g))) if mode==-1 else float(np.max(g))
        if not finite or scale <=1e-300: ... break
        d=g/scale
        factors=(0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0)
        best_cand_score=score; best_cand=None; chosen=step
        for fac in factors:
            st=step*fac
            if st<=0: continue
            zt=z - st*d
            if fail>12:
                zt = zt + (0.03+0.02*min(1,fail/100))*rng.standard_normal(n)
            zt=_center_clip_z(zt)
            at=_softmax_target(zt,1.0)
            ct=_conv_fft(at,conv_size)
            sc=_score_from_c(at,ct)
            if sc<best_cand_score:
               best_cand_score=sc; best_cand=(zt,at,ct); chosen=st
        if best_cand is not None and best_cand_score < score - 1e-12:
            z,a,c=best_cand; score=best_cand_score
            step=min(2.0, max(0.02, chosen*1.08))
            fail=0
            if score < best_score:
                best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); best_z=z.copy(); print occasionally
        else:
            step*=0.7
            fail +=1
            if step <0.015: step=0.25
            if fail>30:
                # reset
                sigma=0.15 + 0.3*min(1.0, fail/200.0)
                z=best_z.copy() + sigma*rng.standard_normal(n)
                # occasionally large random
                if fail>100 and rng.random()<0.1:
                    z=rng.standard_normal(n)*0.5
                z=_center_clip_z(z)
                a=_softmax_target(z,1.0)
                c=_conv_fft(a,conv_size)
                score=_score_from_c(a,c)
                step=0.25
                fail=0
        # if fail > 200 break?
        if fail>300: break
    return best_a,best_score
```

Main schedule detailed:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=400, **kwargs):
 rng=np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
 deadline=time.monotonic()+max(5.0, float(budget_s)-5.0)
 best_seq=_sqrt_series(4096); best_score=_local_score(best_seq)
 # height
 height=_get_height()
 if height: scoreH=local; if finite: add; update global.
 candidates=[]
 heights maybe add.
 # starts for lengths
 lengths=[]
 if budget_s>60: lengths=[4096,8192]
 if budget_s>180 and budget_s<=400: lengths.append(16384)? maybe if time.
 for n in lengths:
    cands=_make_start_candidates(n,rng)
    candidates.extend(cands)
    # lowdim for n if remaining > 60 and (n<=8192 or time large)
    if time < deadline-60:
       t_low=min(20.0, (deadline-time)*0.08)
       seqL,scoreL=_lowdim_search(n,t_low,rng,'low')
       if scoreL finite: candidates append.
 # Sort candidates by score, remove duplicates? duplicates not issue.
 candidates.sort(key=score)
 # Select top for full optimize: want diversity lengths. selection=[]
 for cand in candidates:
    if score==inf continue
    if len(selection)>=10: break

```
import numpy as np
import time

def _next_p2(m):
    m=int(m)
    if m<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```
Bit_length of int(m-1) works. For m=2, (1).bit_length=1 ->1<<1=2. For m=3,2.bit_length=2 ->4.

_ normalize:
```
def _normalize_sequence(a, target=1.0):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(a.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-300:
       a=np.ones(a.size,dtype=np.float64)
       s=float(a.size)
    if target>0 and s>0:
       a=a*(target/s)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    return a
```
If target=1 and a size large, values okay.

_sqrt_series:
```
def _sqrt_series(n):
   n=int(n); a=np.empty(n); if n==0 return a; a[0]=1; for k in range(1,n): a[k]=a[k-1]*(2*k-1)/(2*k); return a
```

_softmax_target:
```
def _softmax_target(z, target=1.0):
   z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64)
   if z.size==0: return np.array([])
   z=np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -1e300)
   z=z-np.max(z)
   ex=np.exp(z)
   s=float(ex.sum(dtype=np.float64))
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
       return np.full(z.shape, target/z.size)
   return target*ex/s
```

_center_clip_z:
```
def _center_clip_z(z):
   z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64)
   if z.size==0: return z
   z=np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
   z=z-np.mean(z)
   mn=float(np.min(z)); mx=float(np.max(z)); rng=mx-mn
   if not np.isfinite(rng) or rng<=0: return z
   if rng>60.0: z=z*(60.0/rng)
   return z
```

_conv_fft:
```
def _conv_fft(a, size):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
   n=a.size
   out_len=2*n-1
   size=max(size,_next_p2(out_len))
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
   C=A*A
   out=np.fft.irfft(C,size)[:out_len]
   out=np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
   # numerical negative
   out=np.maximum(out,0.0)
   return out
```

_score_from_c:
```
def _score_from_c(a,c):
   n=a.size
   if n==0 or c.size==0: return np.inf
   S=float(a.sum(dtype=np.float64))
   if not np.isfinite(S) or S<=1e-300: return np.inf
   cmax=float(np.max(c))
   if not np.isfinite(cmax): return np.inf
   if cmax<=0: cmax=0.0
   return float(2.0*n*cmax/(S*S))
```
Since a sum already 1, but ok.

_local_score: calls normalize? It should not modify? Use `a=_normalize_sequence(a,1.0)` maybe. If a sum not exactly 1 due modifications, normalizing changes score? scale invariant so okay. But if a has tiny zeros, fine.
```
def _local_score(a):
   a=_normalize_sequence(a,1.0)
   if a.size==0: return inf
   if a.size>40000: # maybe too slow? but local functions handle? We can still.
   csize=_next_p2(2*a.size-1)
   c=_conv_fft(a,csize)
   return _score_from_c(a,c)
```

_gradient:
```
def _gradient(a, weights, grad_size):
   n=a.size; L=weights.size
   rw=weights[::-1]
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,grad_size)
   W=np.fft.rfft(rw,grad_size)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(A*W,grad_size)
   start=L-1; stop=start-n
   r=conv[start:stop:-1]
   if r.size!=n:
      r=conv[max(0,L-n):L]
      r=r[-n:]
   g=2.0*r
   g=np.where(np.isfinite(g),g,0.0)
   return g
```
If weights length maybe 0? not.

_peak_weights:
```
def _peak_weights(c, q):
   L=c.size
   cmax=float(np.max(c)) if L else 0
   if L==0: return np.array([])
   if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax<=0: return np.ones(L)/L
   rel=c/cmax
   rel=np.where(np.isfinite(rel)&(rel>0), rel, 0.0)
   if q<=1:
      w=rel
      s=float(w.sum())
      if s<=0: idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w=np.zeros(L); w[idx]=1
      else: w/=s
      return w
   logw=np.full(L,-np.inf)
   mask=rel>0
   if not np.any(mask): idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w=zeros; w[idx]=1; return w
   logw[mask]=float(q)*np.log(rel[mask])
   logw=logw - float(np.max(logw))
   w=np.exp(logw)
   w=np.where(np.isfinite(w),w,0.0)
   s=float(w.sum())
   if s<=0: idx=argmax(c); w=zeros; w[idx]=1; return w
   return w/s
```
For q=1, if rel all zeros? cmax positive so at least one. Good.

_l2_weights:
```
def _l2_weights(c, quantile=50.0):
   target=float(np.mean(c)) # or percentile
   w=c - target
   w=np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
   # optional scale
   return w
```
Using mean; if all zero, scale 0.

def _make_start_candidates(n, rng):
    cands=[]
    n=int(n)
    t=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
    if n>1: t=t/float(n-1)
    else: t=np.zeros(1)
    # helper local? no, call _append_candidate
    seqs=[]
    seqs.append(_sqrt_series(n)); seqs.append(_sqrt_series(n)[::-1].copy())
    # powers
    for p in [0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0]:
       seqs.append(np.power(t+1.0, -p)); seqs.append(np.power((1.0-t)+1.0, -p))
    # exponentials/logistic
    for c in [-2.0,-1.0,1.0,2.0]:
       seqs.append(np.exp(c*t)); seqs.append(np.exp(c*(1.0-t)))
    # beta subset
    eps=0.02
    base_sqrt=_sqrt_series(n)
    if n <=8192:
       for p in [0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.5]:
          for q in [0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.5]:
             seqs.append(np.power(t+eps, -p) * np.power(1.0-t+eps, -q))
    else:
       for p,q in [(0.5,0.5),(0.75,0.5),(0.5,0.75),(1.0,0.5),(0.5,1.0)]: ...
    # modulated sqrt
    for amp in [0.3,0.6,1.0,1.5]:
       seqs.append(base_sqrt*(1.0 + amp*t))
       seqs.append(base_sqrt*(1.0 + amp*(2.0*t-1.0)**2))
       seqs.append(base_sqrt*(1.0 + amp*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*t)))
       seqs.append(base_sqrt*(1.0 + amp*np.sin(4.0*np.pi*t)))
    # random smooth 5
    for _ in range(5):
       z=np.log(np.maximum(base_sqrt,1e-300))
       z=z + 0.5*rng.standard_normal(n) ??? elementwise rough; smooth? Use low pass:
       noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
       noise = np.convolve(noise, np.ones(7)/7, mode='same') # but direct n small okay; for 16k still okay.
       z = z + 0.5*noise
       seqs.append(_softmax_target(_center_clip_z(z),1.0))
    for s in seqs:
       _append_candidate(cands,s,n)
       # could stop if too many? _append limits 80
    return cands
```

Resample height:
```
def _resample_array(arr,n):
    arr=np.asarray(arr).ravel(); m=arr.size
    if m==0: return np.ones(1)
    if m==n: return arr.copy()
    pos=np.linspace(0.0,m,n)
    left=np.floor(pos).astype(int); right=np.minimum(left+1,m-1); left=np.minimum(left,m-1)
    frac=pos-left
    out=(1-frac)*arr[left]+frac*arr[right]
    return np.maximum(out,0)
```
If m=1, pos all 0.

_get_height_sequence:
```
def _get_height_sequence():
    try: h=height_sequence_1
    except NameError: return None
    try: arr=np.asarray(h,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except: return None
    if arr.size==0: return None
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0); arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
    if arr.size>20000: return None # maybe too slow
    return arr
```

Lowdim basis and search:
```
def _lowdim_basis(n):
    t=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
    if n>1: t=t/(n-1)
    else: t=np.zeros(1)
    basis=[]
    basis.append(2.0*t-1.0)
    basis.append((t-0.5)*(t-0.5)*2.0)
    basis.append(np.sin(2.0*np.pi*t))
    basis.append(np.sin(4.0*np.pi*t))
    basis.append(np.cos(2.0*np.pi*t))
    basis.append(np.exp(-((t-0.25)/0.12)**2))
    basis.append(np.exp(-((t-0.75)/0.12)**2))
    basis.append(np.exp(-((t-0.10)/0.08)**2))
    basis.append(np.exp(-((t-0.90)/0.08)**2))
    # maybe limit to 8
    return basis[:8]
```

_seq_from_basis:
```
def _seq_from_basis(z0, basis, params):
    z=z0.copy()
    for j in range(len(basis)):
       z += float(params[j])*basis[j]
    z=_center_clip_z(z)
    return _softmax_target(z,1.0)
```

_eval_basis maybe not needed; call local score.

_lowdim_search:
```
def _lowdim_search(n, max_seconds, rng, label, deadline):
    start=time.monotonic()
    stop=min(start+max_seconds, deadline)
    base=_sqrt_series(n)
    z0=np.log(np.maximum(base,1e-300)); z0=_center_clip_z(z0)
    basis=_lowdim_basis(n)
    K=len(basis)
    best_seq=None; best_score=np.inf
    improved=0
    while time.monotonic() < stop - 1.0:
        params=np.zeros(K); params[:]=rng.normal(0.0,0.6,K)
        params=np.clip(params,-5.0,5.0)
        seq=_seq_from_basis(z0,basis,params)
        score=_local_score(seq)
        if not np.isfinite(score): params=zeros? continue
        local_params=params.copy(); local_score=score; step=0.5
        if score<best_score: best_score=score; best_seq=seq.copy(); improved+=1
        inner_stop=min(time.monotonic()+max(2.0, (stop-time.monotonic())*0.2), stop-0.5)
        noimp=0
        while time.monotonic() < inner_stop:
           pert=local_params + step*rng.standard_normal(K)
           pert=np.clip(pert,-8.0,8.0)
           seq2=_seq_from_basis(z0,basis,pert)
           sc2=_local_score(seq2)
           if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < local_score*(1.0-1e-10):
               local_params=pert.copy(); local_score=sc2; noimp=0; step=min(1.5, step*1.07)
               if sc2<best_score: best_score=sc2; best_seq=seq2.copy(); improved+=1
           else:
               noimp+=1; step*=0.85
               if step<0.02:
                   local_params=best? use params? reset to np.clip(rng.normal(0,0.8,K),-5,5); score local...
           if noimp>80: break
        if improved>6: break? maybe after several improvements.
    if best_seq is None: best_seq=base; best_score=_local_score(base)
    return _normalize_sequence(best_seq,1.0), best_score
```
The inner loop could be too many evals if time left. `inner_stop` is 20% of remaining; okay. If n large, _local_score each maybe 2ms; 2s ->1000 eval. Fine.

But if no improvement, lowdim may waste time. Limit max_seconds 15/20. Good.

try_sparsify:
```
def _try_sparsify(a, max_seconds maybe):
    a=_normalize_sequence(a,1.0)
    best=a; best_score=_local_score(a)
    n=a.size
    for frac in [0.01,0.02,0.05,0.10,0.20]:
       cnt=int(n*frac)
       if cnt<=0 or cnt>=n: continue
       b=a.copy()
       idx=np.argpartition(b, cnt)[:cnt]
       b[idx]=0.0
       b=_normalize_sequence(b,1.0)
       sc=_local_score(b)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
          best=b; best_score=sc
    return best,best_score
```
Call after optimize. But _local_score recomputes; okay. Could be after each run; n=16384, 5 calls ~ maybe 0.1s.

Mix candidates:
```
def _mix_sequences(a,b,t):
    a=_normalize_sequence(a,1.0); b=_normalize_sequence(b,1.0)
    if a.size!=b.size:
        b=_resample_array(b,a.size)
    mix=(1-t)*a + t*b
    return _normalize_sequence(mix,1.0)
```

Perturb:
```
def _perturb_sequence(a, sigma, rng):
    a=_normalize_sequence(a,1.0)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z=_center_clip_z(z)
    z=z + sigma*rng.standard_normal(a.size)
    return _softmax_target(z,1.0)
```

Let's implement `_random_smooth_sequence(n,rng)`:
```
def _random_smooth_sequence(n, rng):
    n=int(n)
    N=_next_p2(n)
    z=np.fft.fft(np.random? use rng.standard_normal(N))
    # low-pass: multiply freq
    freqs=np.arange(N)
    mask = freqs < max(2, n//16)
    z *= mask
    z = np.fft.ifft(z)
    z = np.real(z)[:n]
    z = _center_clip_z(z)
    return _softmax_target(z,1.0)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time


"""
Search for nonnegative step heights minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

Strategy:
  * Use FFT-based convolutions and a log-softmax parameterization.
  * Start from several analytic/fourier/random shapes, including the stored
    height_sequence_1 if available.
  * Run mirror-descent-like local search with a cycle of peak, L2, and
    signed-flattening subgradients.
  * Perform low-dimensional shape searches, random restarts, mixing, and
    light sparsification.
  * Finalize by calling evaluate_sequence on a few top candidates.
"""


def _next_p2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize_sequence(a, target=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.size)
    if target > 0 and s > 0:
        a = a * (target / s)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a


def _sqrt_series(n):
    n = int(n)
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 0:
        return a
    a[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(1, n):
        a[k] = a[k - 1] * float(2 * k - 1) / float(2 * k)
    return a


def _softmax_target(z, target=1.0):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -1e300)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    ex = np.exp(z)
    s = float(ex.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(z.shape, target / float(z.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return target * ex / s


def _center_clip_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0:
        return z
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    mn = float(np.min(z))
    mx = float(np.max(z))
    rng = mx - mn
    if not np.isfinite(rng) or rng <= 0.0:
        return z
    if rng > 60.0:
        z = z * (60.0 / rng)
    return z


def _conv_fft(a, size):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    out_len = 2 * n - 1
    if out_len <= 0:
        return a.copy()
    size = max(int(size), _next_p2(out_len))
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    C = A * A
    out = np.fft.irfft(C, size)[:out_len]
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return out


def _score_from_c(a, c):
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0 or c.size <= 0:
        return np.inf
    S = float(a.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return np.inf
    if cmax < 0.0:
        cmax = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax / (S * S))


def _local_score(a):
    a = _normalize_sequence(a, 1.0)
    if a.size <= 0:
        return np.inf
    csize = _next_p2(2 * a.size - 1)
    c = _conv_fft(a, csize)
    return _score_from_c(a, c)


def _gradient(a, weights, grad_size):
    n = int(a.size)
    L = int(weights.size)
    if n <= 0 or L <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    rw = weights[::-1]
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, grad_size)
    W = np.fft.rfft(rw, grad_size)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A * W, grad_size)
    start = L - 1
    stop = start - n
    r = conv[start:stop:-1]
    if r.size != n:
        r = conv[max(0, L - n):L]
        r = r[-n:]
    g = 2.0 * r
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    return g


def _peak_weights(c, q):
    L = int(c.size)
    if L <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    rel = c / cmax
    rel = np.where(np.isfinite(rel) & (rel > 0.0), rel, 0.0)
    if q <= 1:
        w = rel.copy()
        s = float(w.sum(dtype=np.float64))
        if s <= 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(c))
            w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            w[idx] = 1.0
        else:
            w /= s
        return w
    logw = np.full(L, -np.inf, dtype=np.float64)
    mask = rel > 0.0
    if not np.any(mask):
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0
        return w
    logw[mask] = float(q) * np.log(rel[mask])
    logw = logw - float(np.max(logw))
    w = np.exp(logw)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    s = float(w.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if s <= 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0
    else:
        w /= s
    return w


def _sign_weights(c):
    L = int(c.size)
    if L <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    med = float(np.median(c))
    w = np.tanh(20.0 * (c - med) / cmax)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    return w


def _append_candidate(cands, seq, n):
    if len(cands) >= 90:
        return
    a = _normalize_sequence(seq, 1.0)
    if a.size <= 0:
        return
    sc = _local_score(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        cands.append((float(sc), a.copy(), int(a.size)))


def _resample_array(arr, n):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = arr.size
    n = int(n)
    if m <= 0 or n <= 0:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    if m == n:
        return arr.copy()
    pos = np.linspace(0.0, float(m), n)
    left = np.floor(pos).astype(int)
    right = np.minimum(left + 1, m - 1)
    left = np.minimum(left, m - 1)
    frac = pos - left
    out = (1.0 - frac) * arr[left] + frac * arr[right]
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def _get_height_sequence():
    try:
        h = height_sequence_1
    except NameError:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if arr.size > 20000:
        return None
    return arr


def _random_smooth_sequence(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    z = rng.standard_normal(n)
    k = 11
    w = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / float(k)
    z = np.convolve(z, w, mode="same")
    z = _center_clip_z(z)
    return _softmax_target(z, 1.0)


def _psd_random_sequence(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    N = _next_p2(max(8, n))
    b = rng.standard_normal(N)
    a = np.abs(np.fft.fft(b)) ** 2
    if a.size > n:
        a = a[:n]
    elif a.size < n:
        tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        tmp[:a.size] = a
        a = tmp
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    return _normalize_sequence(a, 1.0)


def _psd_sqrt_sequence(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    N = _next_p2(max(8, n))
    base = _sqrt_series(n)
    phase = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random(n)
    b = np.zeros(N, dtype=complex)
    b[:n] = base * np.exp(1j * phase)
    a = np.abs(np.fft.fft(b)) ** 2
    if a.size > n:
        a = a[:n]
    elif a.size < n:
        tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        tmp[:a.size] = a
        a = tmp
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    return _normalize_sequence(a, 1.0)


def _make_start_candidates(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    cands = []
    if n <= 0:
        return cands

    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n > 1:
        t = t / float(n - 1)
    else:
        t = np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)

    large = n > 10000

    base_sqrt = _sqrt_series(n)

    seqs = []
    seqs.append(base_sqrt)
    seqs.append(base_sqrt[::-1].copy())

    for p in [0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0]:
        seqs.append(np.power(t + 1.0, -p))
        seqs.append(np.power((1.0 - t) + 1.0, -p))

    for c in [-2.0, -1.0, 1.0, 2.0]:
        seqs.append(np.exp(c * t))
        seqs.append(np.exp(c * (1.0 - t)))

    if not large:
        eps = 0.02
        for p in [0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5]:
            for q in [0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5]:
                seqs.append(
                    np.power(t + eps, -p) * np.power((1.0 - t) + eps, -q)
                )
    else:
        eps = 0.02
        for p, q in [(0.5, 0.5), (0.5, 0.75), (0.75, 0.5), (0.5, 1.0), (1.0, 0.5)]:
            seqs.append(
                np.power(t + eps, -p) * np.power((1.0 - t) + eps, -q)
            )

    for amp in [-0.6, -0.3, 0.3, 0.6, 1.0, 1.8]:
        fac = 1.0 + amp * t
        fac = np.maximum(0.05, fac)
        seqs.append(base_sqrt * fac)

    for amp in [0.3, 0.7, 1.3, 2.0]:
        fac = 1.0 + amp * (2.0 * t - 1.0) ** 2
        seqs.append(base_sqrt * fac)
        fac = 1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t)
        fac = np.maximum(0.05, fac)
        seqs.append(base_sqrt * fac)
        fac = 1.0 + amp * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * t)
        fac = np.maximum(0.05, fac)
        seqs.append(base_sqrt * fac)

    for _ in range(3 if not large else 1):
        z = np.log(np.maximum(base_sqrt, 1e-300))
        r = rng.standard_normal(n)
        k = 15
        r = np.convolve(r, np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / float(k), mode="same")
        z = z + 0.55 * r
        seqs.append(_softmax_target(_center_clip_z(z), 1.0))

    if n <= 8192:
        for _ in range(4 if not large else 2):
            seqs.append(_psd_random_sequence(n, rng))
        seqs.append(_psd_sqrt_sequence(n, rng))

    for s in seqs:
        _append_candidate(cands, s, n)

    return cands


def _candidate_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _try_sparsify(a, deadline=np.inf):
    a = _normalize_sequence(a, 1.0)
    if a.size <= 2:
        return a, _local_score(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _local_score(a)
    n = a.size
    for frac in [0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20]:
        if time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
            break
        cnt = int(n * frac)
        if cnt <= 0 or cnt >= n:
            continue
        b = a.copy()
        idx = np.argpartition(b, cnt)[:cnt]
        b[idx] = 0.0
        b = _normalize_sequence(b, 1.0)
        sc = _local_score(b)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = b.copy()
            best_score = sc
    return best, best_score


def _lowdim_basis(n):
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n > 1:
        t = t / float(n - 1)
    else:
        t = np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    basis = []
    basis.append(2.0 * t - 1.0)
    basis.append(2.0 * (t - 0.5) ** 2)
    basis.append(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t))
    basis.append(np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * t))
    basis.append(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t))
    basis.append(np.exp(-((t - 0.25) / 0.12) ** 2))
    basis.append(np.exp(-((t - 0.75) / 0.12) ** 2))
    basis.append(np.exp(-((t - 0.10) / 0.08) ** 2))
    basis.append(np.exp(-((t - 0.90) / 0.08) ** 2))
    return basis[:8]


def _seq_from_basis(z0, basis, params):
    z = z0.copy()
    for j in range(len(basis)):
        z = z + float(params[j]) * basis[j]
    z = _center_clip_z(z)
    return _softmax_target(z, 1.0)


def _lowdim_search(n, max_seconds, rng, label, deadline):
    start = time.monotonic()
    stop = min(start + float(max_seconds), float(deadline))
    base = _sqrt_series(n)
    z0 = np.log(np.maximum(base, 1e-300))
    z0 = _center_clip_z(z0)
    basis = _lowdim_basis(n)
    K = len(basis)

    best_seq = base.copy()
    best_score = _local_score(best_seq)
    improved = 0

    while time.monotonic() < stop - 1.0:
        params = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
        params[:] = rng.normal(0.0, 0.6, K)
        params = np.clip(params, -5.0, 5.0)

        seq = _seq_from_basis(z0, basis, params)
        score = _local_score(seq)
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            continue

        local_params = params.copy()
        local_score = score
        step = 0.45
        noimp = 0

        if score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            improved += 1

        inner_stop = min(
            time.monotonic() + max(2.0, 0.22 * max(0.0, stop - time.monotonic())),
            stop - 0.5,
        )

        while time.monotonic() < inner_stop:
            pert = local_params + step * rng.standard_normal(K)
            pert = np.clip(pert, -8.0, 8.0)
            seq2 = _seq_from_basis(z0, basis, pert)
            sc2 = _local_score(seq2)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < local_score * (1.0 - 1e-10):
                local_params = pert.copy()
                local_score = sc2
                noimp = 0
                step = min(1.5, step * 1.07)
                if sc2 < best_score:
                    best_score = sc2
                    best_seq = seq2.copy()
                    improved += 1
            else:
                noimp += 1
                step *= 0.85
                if step < 0.02:
                    local_params = np.clip(rng.normal(0.0, 0.8, K), -5.0, 5.0)
                    local_score = _local_score(_seq_from_basis(z0, basis, local_params))
            if noimp > 90:
                break

        if improved >= 8:
            break

    out = _normalize_sequence(best_seq, 1.0)
    return out, float(best_score)


def _optimize_run(a_init, max_seconds, rng, label, deadline):
    a0 = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a0.size <= 0:
        a0 = _sqrt_series(4096)
    n = int(a0.size)
    if n <= 1:
        a = _normalize_sequence(a0, 1.0)
        return a, _local_score(a)

    run_start = time.monotonic()
    run_stop = min(run_start + float(max_seconds), float(deadline))
    if run_stop - run_start <= 0.5:
        a = _normalize_sequence(a0, 1.0)
        return a, _local_score(a)

    conv_size = _next_p2(2 * n - 1)
    grad_size = _next_p2(3 * n - 2)

    a = _normalize_sequence(a0, 1.0)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    z = _center_clip_z(z)
    a = _softmax_target(z, 1.0)
    c = _conv_fft(a, conv_size)
    score = _score_from_c(a, c)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score
    best_z = z.copy()

    if not np.isfinite(score):
        a = _sqrt_series(n)
        z = _center_clip_z(np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300)))
        a = _softmax_target(z, 1.0)
        c = _conv_fft(a, conv_size)
        score = _score_from_c(a, c)
        best_a, best_score, best_z = a.copy(), score, z.copy()

    try:
        print(f"[{label}] n={n} start={best_score:.9f}", flush=True)
    except Exception:
        pass

    step = 0.25
    fail = 0
    mode_cycle = [-1, 1, -2, 4, -1, 8, -2, 16, -1, 32, -2, 64]
    mode_idx = 0
    phase_end = time.monotonic() + 3.0
    last_print = 0.0

    while time.monotonic() < run_stop:
        if time.monotonic() >= phase_end:
            mode_idx = (mode_idx + 1) % len(mode_cycle)
            phase_end = time.monotonic() + (3.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else 5.0)

        mode = mode_cycle[mode_idx]

        cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 1e-300:
            break

        if mode == -1:
            weights = c - float(np.mean(c))
        elif mode == -2:
            weights = _sign_weights(c)
        else:
            weights = _peak_weights(c, int(mode))

        g = _gradient(a, weights, grad_size)
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(g))) if g.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-300:
            break

        d = g / scale

        factors = (0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0)
        best_cand_score = score
        best_cand = None
        chosen_step = step

        for fac in factors:
            st = step * float(fac)
            if st <= 0.0:
                continue
            zt = z - st * d
            if fail > 8:
                nsig = 0.02 + 0.03 * min(1.0, float(fail) / 100.0)
                zt = zt + nsig * rng.standard_normal(n)
            zt = _center_clip_z(zt)
            at = _softmax_target(zt, 1.0)
            ct = _conv_fft(at, conv_size)
            sc = _score_from_c(at, ct)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_cand_score:
                best_cand_score = sc
                best_cand = (zt, at, ct)
                chosen_step = st

        if best_cand is not None and best_cand_score < score - 1e-12:
            z, a, c = best_cand
            score = best_cand_score
            step = min(3.0, max(0.02, chosen_step * 1.08))
            fail = 0
            if score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_z = z.copy()
                now = time.monotonic()
                if now - last_print > 5.0:
                    try:
                        print(f"[{label}] improved best={best_score:.9f}", flush=True)
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                    last_print = now
        else:
            step *= 0.70
            fail += 1
            if step < 0.015:
                step = 0.25
            if fail > 25:
                sigma = 0.18 + 0.42 * min(1.0, float(fail) / 200.0)
                if fail > 100 and rng.random() < 0.08:
                    z = 0.55 * rng.standard_normal(n)
                else:
                    z = best_z.copy() + sigma * rng.standard_normal(n)
                z = _center_clip_z(z)
                a = _softmax_target(z, 1.0)
                c = _conv_fft(a, conv_size)
                score = _score_from_c(a, c)
                step = 0.25
                fail = 0

        if fail > 350:
            break

    out_a, out_score = _try_sparsify(best_a, deadline)
    try:
        print(f"[{label}] finish={out_score:.9f}", flush=True)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return out_a, float(out_score)


def _merge_candidate_pool(cands, new_cands):
    for item in new_cands:
        cands.append(item)


def _finalize_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
        s = 1.0
    a = a / s
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in a]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=400, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    now = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 10.0:
        budget = max(10.0, budget if np.isfinite(budget) else 400.0)

    # Reserve a little time for sparsification and final exact evaluation.
    reserve = 30.0 if budget >= 120.0 else 10.0
    deadline = now + max(5.0, budget - reserve)

    try:
        print(f"budget={budget:.2f}s deadline_reserve={reserve:.2f}s", flush=True)
    except Exception:
        pass

    candidates = []

    # Optional embedded SOTA sequence.
    height = _get_height_sequence()
    height_score = np.inf
    if height is not None:
        _append_candidate(candidates, height, height.size)
        for item in candidates:
            if item[1].size == height.size:
                height_score = item[0]
                break
        for target_n in [4096, 8192, 16384]:
            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
                _append_candidate(candidates, _resample_array(height, target_n), target_n)
                _append_candidate(candidates, _resample_array(height[::-1], target_n), target_n)
        try:
            print(f"height_sequence size={height.size} score={height_score:.9f}", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Fallback.
    fallback = _sqrt_series(4096)
    fallback_score = _local_score(fallback)
    _append_candidate(candidates, fallback, fallback.size)

    best_seq = fallback.copy()
    best_score = fallback_score

    # Candidate generation for several lengths.
    lengths = []
    if budget >= 60.0:
        lengths.extend([4096, 8192])
    if budget >= 180.0:
        lengths.append(16384)

    for n in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20.0:
            break
        start_cands = _make_start_candidates(n, rng)
        _merge_candidate_pool(candidates, start_cands)
        try:
            print(f"length {n}: start candidates {len(start_cands)}", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

        # Low-dimensional shape search, but only with reasonable remaining time.
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
            low_time = min(18.0 if n <= 8192 else 12.0, max(5.0, 0.08 * (deadline - time.monotonic())))
            seqL, scoreL = _lowdim_search(n, low_time, rng, f"low{n}", deadline)
            _append_candidate(candidates, seqL, n)
            if np.isfinite(scoreL):
                try:
                    print(f"lowdim n={n} best={scoreL:.9f}", flush=True)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # Refresh global best from the candidate pool.
    finite_cands = [x for x in candidates if np.isfinite(x[0])]
    if finite_cands:
        finite_cands.sort(key=_candidate_key)
        for sc, seq, n in finite_cands:
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = seq.copy()

    # Select a small set of diverse candidates for full local optimization.
    selected = []
    length_count = {}
    for sc, seq, n in finite_cands:
        if len(selected) >= 8:
            break
        cnt = length_count.get(n, 0)
        if cnt < 3:
            selected.append((sc, seq.copy(), n))
            length_count[n] = cnt + 1

    # Ensure the current global best is in the selected list.
    add_best = True
    for sc, seq, n in selected:
        if seq.size == best_seq.size:
            # Only force if best is not much worse than selected? Keep it if good.
            if best_score <= sc + 1e-9:
                add_best = False
            break
    if add_best:
        selected.insert(0, (best_score, best_seq.copy(), best_seq.size))

    # Full local optimization on selected candidates.
    if selected:
        remaining = float(max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()))
        alloc_total = min(0.72 * remaining, 260.0)
        if alloc_total > 5.0:
            weights = []
            for i in range(len(selected)):
                weights.append(max(0.45, 1.65 - 0.16 * float(i)))
            sw = sum(weights)
            for i, (sc, seq, n) in enumerate(selected):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 15.0:
                    break
                t = alloc_total * float(weights[i]) / max(1e-12, sw)
                t = min(t, float(deadline - time.monotonic() - 1.0))
                if t < 3.0:
                    continue
                seq2, sc2 = _optimize_run(seq, t, rng, f"sel{n}_{i}", deadline)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                    best_score = sc2
                    best_seq = seq2.copy()
                    _append_candidate(candidates, best_seq, best_seq.size)
                    finite_cands = [x for x in candidates if np.isfinite(x[0])]
                    finite_cands.sort(key=_candidate_key)

    # Random/mixing restarts from the current best and good candidates.
    restart_candidates = finite_cands
    restart_candidates.sort(key=_candidate_key)

    sigma = 0.06
    no_improve = 0
    restart_i = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
        run_t = min(32.0, float(deadline - time.monotonic() - 25.0))
        if run_t < 12.0:
            break

        source = best_seq
        if restart_candidates and (restart_i % 3 == 0):
            sc_s, seq_s, n_s = restart_candidates[restart_i % min(5, len(restart_candidates))]
            source = seq_s

        n_run = int(source.size)
        if source.size == best_seq.size:
            mix = 0.35 + 0.10 * (restart_i % 2)
            mixed = (1.0 - mix) * best_seq + mix * source
            pert = _normalize_sequence(mixed, 1.0)
            pert = _softmax_target(_center_clip_z(np.log(np.maximum(pert, 1e-300)) + sigma * rng.standard_normal(n_run)), 1.0)
        else:
            pert = _softmax_target(_center_clip_z(np.log(np.maximum(source, 1e-300)) + sigma * rng.standard_normal(n_run)), 1.0)

        seq2, sc2 = _optimize_run(pert, run_t, rng, f"restart{restart_i}", deadline)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
            best_score = sc2
            best_seq = seq2.copy()
            _append_candidate(candidates, best_seq, best_seq.size)
            finite_cands = [x for x in candidates if np.isfinite(x[0])]
            finite_cands.sort(key=_candidate_key)
            no_improve = 0
            sigma = max(0.03, sigma * 0.82)
            try:
                print(f"restart {restart_i} improved to {best_score:.9f}", flush=True)
            except Exception:
                pass
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            sigma = min(1.2, sigma * 1.35)

        restart_i += 1

    # Final exact evaluation on a few top candidates.
    candidates_finite = [x for x in candidates if np.isfinite(x[0])]
    candidates_finite.sort(key=_candidate_key)

    eval_list = [best_seq]
    for sc, seq, n in candidates_finite:
        if len(eval_list) >= 3:
            break
        same_size = False
        for existing in eval_list:
            if existing.size == seq.size:
                same_size = True
                break
        if not same_size:
            eval_list.append(seq.copy())

    limit = 3
    has_large = False
    for seq in eval_list:
        if seq.size > 12000:
            has_large = True
    if has_large:
        limit = 2

    best_exact_seq = _finalize_sequence(best_seq)
    best_exact_score = np.inf
    exact_calls = 0

    for seq in eval_list[:limit]:
        if exact_calls >= limit:
            break
        lst = _finalize_sequence(seq)
        try:
            sc = evaluate_sequence(lst)
        except Exception:
            sc = np.inf
        exact_calls += 1
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact_score:
            best_exact_score = sc
            best_exact_seq = lst

    if not np.isfinite(best_exact_score):
        best_exact_seq = _finalize_sequence(best_seq)

    try:
        print(
            f"final length={len(best_exact_seq)} "
            f"local_best={best_score:.9f} exact_best={best_exact_score:.9f}"
        )
    except Exception:
        pass

    return best_exact_seq
```
